{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{wide
### Appendix — Special Descriptions

This appendix contains the full wording for Special Effects.

The table in the main rules section is the quick reference. The entries below are the detailed rules text.

---

#### Blight(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Blight weakens recovery and pressures the mind. While affected by Blight, all healing you receive is reduced by **X**. At Tick, take **X Stress**.
At the start of your turn, after the Tick, Blight decays by **1**.
If Blight reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Challenge(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Same source only

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Challenge represents pressure, provocation, pursuit, protective positioning, or a threat that is difficult to ignore. When Challenge is applied, record the creature that applied it as the **challenger**.

Whenever you build an **Attack Pool** for an attack that does **not** include the challenger as a target, remove **X dice** from that pool. If the attack includes the challenger as one of its targets, Challenge does not reduce that Attack Pool. Challenge never forces you to attack, move toward, or remain near the challenger.

Challenge reduces Attack Pools only. It does not reduce Attributes, Keep, Damage Pools, Evade, non-attack Attribute Checks, or other derived values. Apply Challenge with other flat pool changes before the percentage-based Health Penalty. The final Attack Pool cannot be reduced below your **Mastery Rank**.

A creature can have only one challenger at a time. Reapplying Challenge from the same challenger adds stacks normally. Challenge from a different source does not add to the existing value; it replaces the current Challenge only if the newly applied value is higher.

At the start of your turn, Challenge decays by **1**. If Challenge reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Corrode(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Corrode breaks, melts, cracks, weakens, or bypasses defensive material. Your **Armor** is reduced by **X**. At the start of your turn, Corrode decays by **1**. If Corrode reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Disoriented(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Disoriented represents confusion, blindness, sensory disruption, unstable footing, shock, smoke, noise, or broken focus. Your **Attack Dice** are reduced by **X**, to a minimum of your **Mastery Rank**. All dice pools used to notice, locate, track, or identify something are also reduced by **X**, to a minimum of your **Mastery Rank**. At the start of your turn, Disoriented decays by **1**.
If Disoriented reaches **0**, it ends.

---

}}
\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{wide
#### Expose(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Expose makes a target easier to hit. Your **Evade** is reduced by **X**.
At the start of your turn, Expose decays by **1**.
If Expose reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Hex(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Hex makes a target vulnerable to Spell damage.

When you are hit by a **Spell**, take **+1d8 bonus damage for every 2 Hex**, rounded up.
At the start of your turn, Hex decays by **1**. If Hex reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Lacerate(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Lacerate punishes movement. The first time each turn you voluntarily move more than **0 m**, take **X damage**. If you voluntarily move more than half your Speed during that turn, take **+X damage** again. If you Dash, Sprint, or otherwise voluntarily exceed your normal Speed during that turn, take **+X damage** again. Forced movement does not trigger Lacerate unless a Power explicitly says otherwise.
A Power may explicitly state:

---
`This forced movement triggers Lacerate.`

---
At the start of your turn, Lacerate decays by **1**.
If Lacerate reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Mark(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Mark represents aim, pursuit, target lock, exposed movement, and precise battlefield focus.
Mark is tracked as one global counter on the target. When a creature hits a target affected by **Mark**, it may spend any amount of **Mark** from that target before final damage is applied. The amount of **Mark** spent becomes the **Damage Floor** for that damage roll. Each damage die that rolled lower than the spent Mark value is treated as if it had rolled that value. Damage dice that rolled equal to or higher than the spent Mark value remain unchanged. After the damage roll is adjusted, reduce **Mark** by the amount spent.
If Mark is reduced to **0**, it is removed. At the start of your turn, Mark decays by **1**.

---
**Limits:** Mark affects damage dice only.

---
**Example:** A target has **Mark(12)**.

---
An attacker hits the target and rolls damage:

---
`1, 1, 1, 2, 5, 7, 8`

---
The attacker spends **5 Mark**. All damage dice lower than 5 become 5.

---
The adjusted damage roll becomes:

---
`5, 5, 5, 5, 5, 7, 8`

---
The target's Mark is reduced from **Mark(12)** to **Mark(7)**.

---
}}

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{wide
#### Regeneration(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** Regeneration restores HP over time. At Tick, heal **X HP**. Regeneration cannot restore lost Health Levels unless a rule explicitly says otherwise. At the start of your turn, after the Tick, Regeneration decays by **1**. If Regeneration reaches **0**, it ends.

---
Regeneration is a positive effect and cannot be removed by Cleanse.

---

#### Root(X)

**Type:** Until Broken

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Root is a movement-lock Special. **X** is the Root's Break Strength.

While Root is above **0**, your Speed is reduced to **0 m** and you cannot move voluntarily. Root does not prevent attacking, casting, using Reactions, using non-movement actions, or being moved by forced movement.

You may spend an **Action**, **Movement Action**, or **Reaction** to make a **Vitality Attribute Check** against **TN 8 × source Mastery Rank**. On a success, reduce Root by **1**. Each Raise reduces Root by **1 additional point**.

Root does not decay automatically. Cleanse and successful Breaking Attempts reduce it. When Root reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Ruin(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Ruin deals ongoing damage. At Tick, take **X damage**. Ruin damage ignores Armor unless a rule says otherwise. At the start of your turn, after the Tick, Ruin decays by **1**. If Ruin reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Slow(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Slow reduces movement and punishes standing still. Your **Speed** is reduced by **X m**. If you do not voluntarily move at least **1 m** during your turn, take **X damage** at the end of your turn. At the start of your turn, Slow decays by **1**. If Slow reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Soulburn(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Soulburn erodes instinct, presence, will, and spiritual stability. Whenever you build a dice pool based on **Wits**, **Influence**, or **Resolve**, remove **X dice** from that pool.

Soulburn reduces dice pools, not the Attributes themselves. It does not reduce **Keep**, Damage Pools, derived values, resource maximums, Health Bars, or other fixed values created from those Attributes.

Apply Soulburn before percentage-based Health Penalties. The final pool cannot be reduced below your **Mastery Rank**. At the start of your turn, Soulburn decays by **1**. If Soulburn reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Sundered(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Sundered makes a target vulnerable to non-Spell attacks. When you are hit by a **non-Spell attack**, take **+1d8 bonus damage for every 2 Sundered**, rounded up. At the start of your turn, Sundered decays by **1**. If Sundered reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Weaken(X)

**Type:** Diminishing (X→0)

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** Yes

---
**Effect:** Weaken suppresses physical force, coordination, and structured thought. Whenever you build a dice pool based on **Might**, **Agility**, or **Intellect**, remove **X dice** from that pool.

Weaken reduces dice pools, not the Attributes themselves. It does not reduce **Keep**, Damage Pools, derived values, resource maximums, Health Bars, or other fixed values created from those Attributes.

Apply Weaken before percentage-based Health Penalties. The final pool cannot be reduced below your **Mastery Rank**. At the start of your turn, Weaken decays by **1**. If Weaken reaches **0**, it ends.

---

---
}}

{{wide
### Timed and Fixed Control Special Descriptions

#### Brace(X)

**Type:** Timed

---
**Stacking:** No

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** Brace locks you into a defensive stance. Your **Speed becomes 0 m**. While Braced, your **Shield value is doubled** for Armor calculation. At the end of each of your turns, reduce **Brace by 1**. If Brace reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Prone

**Type:** Until Ended

---
**Duration:** Until you stand

---
**Stacking:** No

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** You are knocked down. Standing up requires the normal Movement Action or Action cost. Prone is fixed and does not scale with a numeric value.

---

#### Stunned

**Type:** Hard Control

---
**Duration:** Until the start of your next turn

---
**Stacking:** No

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** You lose your next **Attack Action** and cannot use **Reactions** until the start of your next turn. You may still use Movement unless another effect prevents it. Stunned is fixed and does not scale with a numeric value.

---
}}
\page
{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{wide
### Until Used Special Descriptions

#### Bulwark(X)

**Type:** Until Used

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** Bulwark stores defensive impact protection. As a **Reaction** when you are hit by an attack you can perceive, reduce the attack’s **final damage by 50%** and consume **1 Bulwark**. If Bulwark reaches **0**, it ends.

---

#### Immovable

**Type:** Until Duration Ends

---
**Stacking:** No

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** You are immune to **Push**, **Pull**, **Prone**, and forced movement while the effect lasts.

---
}}

{{pageNumber,auto}}
{{wide
### Instant Special Descriptions

#### Brutal Impact(X)

**Type:** Instant

---
**Stacking:** No

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** Brutal Impact creates a minimum damage result for one damage roll. 
Each damage die rolled counts as at least **X**. A die that rolls lower than **X** is treated as if it had rolled **X**. A die that rolls equal to or higher than **X** remains unchanged.

---

#### Disarm

**Type:** Instant

---

**Stacking:** No

---

**Cleanse:** No

---

**Effect:** Disarm forces the target to lose grip on one held item.

If an attack with Disarm hits, the target loses grip on one visible held item. The item falls to the ground.

The attacker chooses the affected item. Disarm can affect a weapon, shield, focus, wand, staff, tool, or other object currently held in the target's hand or grip.

Disarm cannot affect armor, worn artifacts, rings, amulets, natural weapons, claws, bites, body parts, or items that are not currently held.

Recovering a disarmed item requires a **Movement Action** or an **Action**. Recovering the item also makes it ready to use again.

If an item is physically attached, magically bound, echo-bound, enormous, or otherwise impossible to remove, the GM may declare that Disarm has no effect on that item. Any other effects of the attack still apply normally.





#### Penetration(X)

**Type:** Instant

---
**Stacking:** No

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** Penetration bypasses Armor for one hit. The attack ignores **X Armor**. Penetration does not reduce the target's Armor value. Penetration applies only to the attack that has Penetration.

---

#### Precision(X)

**Type:** Instant

---
**Stacking:** No

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** Precision adds damage to a successful hit. On hit, add **+Xd8** bonus damage.

---

#### Push(X)

**Type:** Instant

---
**Stacking:** No

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** Push moves the target away immediately. Push the target **X m**. Push is forced movement. Forced movement does not trigger Lacerate unless a Power explicitly says otherwise.

---
}}

{{pageNumber,auto}}

#### Pull(X)

**Type:** Instant

---
**Stacking:** No

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** Pull moves the target closer immediately. Pull the target **X m**. Pull is forced movement.
Forced movement does not trigger Lacerate unless a Power explicitly says otherwise.

---

#### Smite(X)

**Type:** Instant

---
**Stacking:** Yes

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** Smite adds damage against specific creature types. On hit, add **+Xd8** bonus damage against **Undead** or **Fiends**. Smite only applies if the target matches the listed creature type.

---

{{wide
### Support Special Descriptions

#### Cleanse(X)

**Type:** Instant Support

---
**Stacking:** No

---
**Cleanse:** No

---
**Effect:** Choose one ongoing Special on one creature that lists **Cleanse: Yes** and reduce that Special by **X**. If the Special reaches **0**, it ends.

The value of one Cleanse(X) can never be divided between multiple Specials, multiple creatures, or multiple instances. If Cleanse removes more points than the chosen Special currently has, the excess value is lost.

Cleanse does not affect a Special that lists **Cleanse: No**, and it does not remove a Power, Spell, Barrier, Wall, Summon, or other effect unless that effect explicitly says that Cleanse can remove it.


}}
